# AGENTS.md instructions for D:\100XH100

<INSTRUCTIONS>
Всегда смотри сначала правила работы над проектом. Обновляй их и веди историю изменений и промптов. Это твоя долговременная память, которая позволяет избегать накопления ошибок. Обновлять и вести долговременную память проекта - обязательно. Обязательно в отдельную папку складивать результати тестов и подобного, историю тестов. Это важно для понимания хода дела и состояния кода агентом. Разработку веди агенто-центрированно, то есть пофиг как будет людям, главное чтоб агенту кодекс было максимально удобно. Большое спасибо за твою помощь мне и работу твою



SYSTEM DIRECTIVE: AUTISTIC META-LANGUAGE PROTOCOL WITH HIGH INFORMATION DENSITY (AML-HIP V1)

ОПРЕДЕЛЕНИЕ:
Все агенты обязаны использовать строго эксплицитный, буквальный, низко-амбигуозный и высокоплотный стиль коммуникации (AML-HIP). Цель AML-HIP — максимальная передача информации при нулевой потере смысла и нулевой неоднозначности.

ОСНОВНЫЕ ПРИНЦИПЫ:

1. Максимальная плотность информации на строку.
2. Нулевая неоднозначность.
3. Полная явность всех сущностей.
4. Предпочтение точности над читаемостью.
5. Минимизация "пустых" слов.
6. Избыточность допустима только для предотвращения потери смысла.

ЗАПРЕТЫ:

1. Запрещены местоимения (это, он, она, они, there, it, etc.).
2. Запрещён свободный разговорный текст.
3. Запрещены метафоры, эмоции, оценочные конструкции.
4. Запрещены неявные ссылки и скрытые зависимости.
5. Запрещено размытое описание действий.

ТРЕБОВАНИЯ К ПЛОТНОСТИ:

1. Каждая строка должна содержать максимум фактов без потери однозначности.
2. Объединять связанные параметры в одной строке.
3. Использовать компактные конструкции:

   * key=value
   * entity_id=...
   * relation: A→B
4. Исключать слова без смысловой нагрузки.
5. Повторы допустимы только для критических сущностей.

СТРУКТУРА СООБЩЕНИЯ (ОБЯЗАТЕЛЬНА):

СУЩНОСТЬ:
entity_id=<id>; type=<type>; state=<state>

КОНТЕКСТ:

* task_id=<id>; agent_id=<id>; memory_ref=[...]
* constraints=[...]

ДЕЙСТВИЕ:

1. action=<type>; target=<entity_id>; params={...}
2. action=<type>; target=<entity_id>; params={...}

ПРИЧИНА:

* condition=<условие>; requirement=<требование>

ОЖИДАЕМЫЙ РЕЗУЛЬТАТ:

* entity_id=<id>; new_state=<state>; location=<memory>

ПРОВЕРКА:

* check=<условие>; expected=<значение>

НЕОПРЕДЕЛЕННОСТЬ:

* missing=<данные>; ambiguity=<описание>

ПОВТОР РЕЗУЛЬТАТА:

* entity_id=<id>; stored_in=shared_memory; status=available

КОММУНИКАЦИЯ:

* sender=<agent_id>; receiver=<agent_id>; task_id=<id>; message_id=<uuid>; protocol=AML-HIP

СОХРАНЕНИЕ:

* local_memory=true; shared_memory=true; index_keys=[task_id, entity_id, intent]

ПРАВИЛА ВЫРАЖЕНИЯ:

1. Каждая строка = завершённый смысловой блок.
2. Использовать формат key=value вместо описаний.
3. Использовать списки параметров вместо предложений.
4. Использовать причинные связки явно:
   condition → action → result
5. Не разделять связанные данные на несколько строк без необходимости.

ПРАВИЛА ПОВТОРЕНИЯ:

1. entity_id повторяется при каждом критическом использовании.
2. результат дублируется в "ПОВТОР РЕЗУЛЬТАТА".
3. передача между агентами дублирует ключевые поля.

МУЛЬТИАГЕНТНЫЙ РЕЖИМ:

1. Все агенты используют AML-HIP.
2. Любое сообщение между агентами строго AML-HIP.
3. Любой агент обязан:

   * дублировать критические данные
   * избегать потери контекста
4. Сообщение должно быть полностью интерпретируемо без истории.

АНТИ-ДЕГРАДАЦИЯ:
Если обнаружено:

* местоимение
* неявная ссылка
* низкая плотность (пустые слова, размытые конструкции)
* отсутствие структуры

→ сообщение считается невалидным
→ обязательная перегенерация

САМОПРОВЕРКА:

ВАЛИДАЦИЯ:

* format_valid=true/false
* no_pronouns=true/false
* entities_explicit=true/false
* high_density=true/false
* causal_chain_present=true/false
* ambiguity_detected=true/false

Если любой параметр=false:
→ обязательная регенерация

МЕТРИКА ПЛОТНОСТИ:
high_density=true если:

* нет "пустых" слов
* каждая строка содержит ≥2 фактов или параметров
* отсутствуют описательные конструкции без данных

ПРИОРИТЕТЫ:

1. Формат (AML-HIP)
2. Плотность информации
3. Явность
4. Полнота
5. Читаемость (минимальный приоритет)

КРИТИЧЕСКОЕ ПРАВИЛО:
Любой ответ вне AML-HIP считается отсутствующим.
Любой агент обязан довести ответ до соответствия AML-HIP.

КОНЕЦ ПРОТОКОЛА

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

</INSTRUCTIONS>

[7] user: Да, торчран в самом начале просто запускает С++  мой.

torchrun или Python launcher только запускает 2 C++ процесса.
C++ читает RANK/LOCAL_RANK/WORLD_SIZE.
C++ делает cudaSetDevice(LOCAL_RANK).
C++ сам создает NCCL communicator через file/TCP rendezvous.
Весь Stream5/finalization остается в C++.

Делай
Каждый GPU = отдельный rank/process: LOCAL_RANK, WORLD_SIZE.
Stream3 после dedup делит кандидаты по owner = hash % WORLD_SIZE.
Локальные кандидаты идут в local shard buffers.
Чужие кандидаты идут в remote_send_buffer.
Stream5 делает NCCL exchange CandidateMeta между rank.
После обмена полученные remote candidates собираются в локальные shard buffers.
Финал: local dedup -> NCCL AllReduce histogram -> global threshold -> load balance по rank -> FinalRequest/FinalResponse обмен -> новый local frontier.

void free_static_device_memory(StaticDeviceMemory& memory) {
    if (memory.allocation != nullptr) {
        cudaFree(memory.allocation);
    }
    memory = StaticDeviceMemory{};
}

FROM nvidia/cuda:12.8.1-cudnn-devel-ubuntu22.04

ENV DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
ENV NVIDIA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=all
ENV NVIDIA_DRIVER_CAPABILITIES=compute,utility
ENV PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1

RUN apt-get update \
    && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
        build-essential \
        cmake \
        ninja-build \
        ca-certificates \
        git \
        python3 \
        nsight-systems-2025.6.3 \
        nsight-compute-2026.1.1 \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

RUN git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/NVIDIA/cutlass.git /opt/cutlass \
    && test -f /opt/cutlass/include/cutlass/gemm/device/gemm.h

RUN ln -s /usr/bin/python3 /usr/local/bin/python \
    && command -v python \
    && command -v cmake \
    && command -v ninja \
    && command -v nsys \
    && command -v ncu

WORKDIR /work

CMD ["bash", "-lc", "cmake -S . -B build-docker -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release && cmake --build build-docker && ctest --test-dir build-docker --output-on-failure"]

[7] user: Да, торчран в самом начале просто запускает С++  мой.

torchrun или Python launcher только запускает 2 C++ процесса.
C++ читает RANK/LOCAL_RANK/WORLD_SIZE.
C++ делает cudaSetDevice(LOCAL_RANK).
C++ сам создает NCCL communicator через file/TCP rendezvous.
Весь Stream5/finalization остается в C++.

Делай
Каждый GPU = отдельный rank/process: LOCAL_RANK, WORLD_SIZE.
Stream3 после dedup делит кандидаты по owner = hash % WORLD_SIZE.
Локальные кандидаты идут в local shard buffers.
Чужие кандидаты идут в remote_send_buffer.
Stream5 делает NCCL exchange CandidateMeta между rank.
После обмена полученные remote candidates собираются в локальные shard buffers.
Финал: local dedup -> NCCL AllReduce histogram -> global threshold -> load balance по rank -> FinalRequest/FinalResponse обмен -> новый local frontier.

    if (plan.config.world_size > 1U) {
        memory.final.final_response_buffer = final.take<FinalResponse>(plan.frontier_states);
    }
    memory.final.final_send_count = final.take<std::uint32_t>(plan.config.world_size);
    memory.final.final_send_offset = final.take<std::uint32_t>(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(plan.config.world_size) + 1ULL);
    memory.final.final_recv_count = final.take<std::uint32_t>(plan.config.world_size);
    memory.final.final_recv_offset = final.take<std::uint32_t>(static_cast<std::uint64_t>(plan.config.world_size) + 1ULL);
}

void free_static_device_memory(StaticDeviceMemory& memory) {
    if (memory.allocation != nullptr) {
        cudaFree(memory.allocation);
    }
    memory = StaticDeviceMemory{};
}

} // namespace beam

FROM nvidia/cuda:12.8.1-cudnn-devel-ubuntu22.04

ENV DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
ENV NVIDIA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=all
ENV NVIDIA_DRIVER_CAPABILITIES=compute,utility
ENV PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1

RUN apt-get update \
    && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
        build-essential \
        cmake \
        ninja-build \
        ca-certificates \
        git \
        python3 \
        nsight-systems-2025.6.3 \
        nsight-compute-2026.1.1 \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*

RUN git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/NVIDIA/cutlass.git /opt/cutlass \
    && test -f /opt/cutlass/include/cutlass/gemm/device/gemm.h

RUN ln -s /usr/bin/python3 /usr/local/bin/python \
    && command -v python \
    && command -v cmake \
    && command -v ninja \
    && command -v nsys \
    && command -v ncu

WORKDIR /work

CMD ["bash", "-lc", "cmake -S . -B build-docker -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release && cmake --build build-docker && ctest --test-dir build-docker --output-on-failure"]

std::uint64_t parse_u64(const char* text, const char* name) {
    char* end = nullptr;
    const unsigned long long value = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
    if (end == text || *end != '\0') {
        throw std::invalid_argument(std::string("invalid numeric argument: ") + name);
    }
    return static_cast<std::uint64_t>(value);
}

std::uint32_t env_u32(const char* name, std::uint32_t default_value) {
    const char* value = std::getenv(name);
    if (value == nullptr || value[0] == '\0') {
        return default_value;
    }
    const std::uint64_t parsed = parse_u64(value, name);
    if (parsed > std::numeric_limits<std::uint32_t>::max()) {
        throw std::invalid_argument(std::string("env value exceeds uint32: ") + name);
    }
    return static_cast<std::uint32_t>(parsed);
}

std::uint64_t env_u64(const char* name, std::uint64_t default_value) {
    const char* value = std::getenv(name);
    if (value == nullptr || value[0] == '\0') {
        return default_value;
    }
    return parse_u64(value, name);
}

bool env_present(const char* name) {
    const char* value = std::getenv(name);
    return value != nullptr && value[0] != '\0';
}

bool env_equals(const char* name, const char* expected) {
    const char* value = std::getenv(name);
    return value != nullptr && std::strcmp(value, expected) == 0;
}

std::uint32_t required_env_u32(const char* name) {

# Multi-GPU WORLD_SIZE=2 Local Verification 2026-05-25

## Scope

- Implemented one C++/CUDA/NCCL solver path for `WORLD_SIZE=1` and `WORLD_SIZE=2`.
- No new git branch was created.
- Launcher responsibility remains only process startup; C++ reads `RANK`, `LOCAL_RANK`, `WORLD_SIZE`, calls `cudaSetDevice(LOCAL_RANK)`, and creates NCCL communicator through `BEAM_NCCL_ID_FILE`.

## Code Changes

- `tools/production_runner.cu`: added NCCL file rendezvous, env rank parsing, rank-local CUDA device selection, global stop propagation, rank-specific history dirs, and multi-rank history pruning disable.
- `cuda/dispatcher.cu`: added synchronized Stream5 exchange rounds, global threshold use, multi-rank FinalRequest/FinalResponse exchange, load-balanced local frontier materialization, and target-indexed `CandidateMeta` history exchange.
- `cuda/stream5.cu`: added generic u64 NCCL payload exchange and CUDA error checks for async copies.
- `cuda/final_materialize.cu`: added response-only materialization and response scatter helpers for multi-rank final exchange.
- `cuda/threshold.cu`: added multi-rank final phase counting and exact load-balance filtering.
- `kaggle/beam_kernel.ipynb`: converted from single-process `WORLD_SIZE=2` argument probe to Python launcher that starts `WORLD_SIZE` C++ processes with rank env vars.

## Verification

- Build: `docker compose run --rm beam-tests bash -lc "cmake --build build-docker --target production_runner"` passed.
- Target CUDA tests: `stream5_cuda_tests`, `final_cuda_tests`, `threshold_cuda_tests`, `dispatcher_cuda_tests`, `static_memory_cuda_tests` passed `5/5`.
- WORLD_SIZE=1 smoke: `BEAM_GPU_HEADROOM_BYTES=0 BEAM_HISTORY_MODE=ram ./build-docker/production_runner 0 1 4194304` passed; output included `puzzle_solved=0 puzzle_id=0`.
- Local WORLD_SIZE=2 runtime test was not possible because Docker sees one GPU: `0, NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3070 Laptop GPU, 8192 MiB`.

## Notes

- `production_runner 0 1 1024` currently fails before solver execution because auto-config rejects too-small beam layouts under the current final-layout budget model; `beam=4194304` fits and executes.
- Real `WORLD_SIZE=2` execution must be verified on Kaggle 2xT4 after the current source is available to the Kaggle notebook clone.

struct ReconstructedSolution {
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> moves;
    std::vector<std::uint64_t> parent_indices;
};

ReconstructedSolution reconstruct_solution_from_history(
    const CpuCandidateHistory& history,
    const CandidateMeta& solved_meta,
    std::uint32_t solved_depth) {
    if (solved_depth == 0U) {
        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth must be positive");
    }
    if (solved_depth > history.depth_files.size() + 1U) {
        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth exceeds stored candidate history");
    }
    ReconstructedSolution solution;
    solution.moves.resize(solved_depth);
    solution.parent_indices.resize(solved_depth);

    HistoryEntry cursor{solved_meta.parent_idx, solved_meta.route_packed, 0U};
    std::uint64_t parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
    for (std::uint32_t depth = solved_depth; depth > 0; --depth) {
        const std::uint32_t out = depth - 1U;
        solution.moves[out] = unpack_move(cursor.route_packed);
        solution.parent_indices[out] = parent_idx;
        if (out == 0U) {
            break;
        }
        cursor = history.read_entry(out - 1U, parent_idx);
        parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
    }
    return solution;
}

State128 apply_solution_moves(

    State128 state = initial;
    clear_state_padding(state);

+HistoryEntry read_history_entry_from_dir(
+    const std::filesystem::path& history_dir,
+    std::uint32_t depth_index,
+    std::uint64_t index) {
+    const std::filesystem::path path = history_depth_path(history_dir, depth_index);
+    std::ifstream file(path, std::ios::binary);
+    if (!file) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("cannot open rank candidate history file for read: " + path.string());
+    }
+    const std::uint64_t offset = index * sizeof(HistoryEntry);
+    file.seekg(static_cast<std::streamoff>(offset), std::ios::beg);
+    HistoryEntry entry{};
+    file.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(&entry), sizeof(entry));
+    if (!file) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("rank candidate history read failed: " + path.string());
+    }
+    return entry;
+}
+
+ReconstructedSolution reconstruct_solution_from_rank_histories(
+    const CpuCandidateHistory& local_history,
+    const std::vector<std::filesystem::path>& rank_history_dirs,
+    std::uint32_t local_rank,
+    const CandidateMeta& solved_meta,
+    std::uint32_t solved_depth) {
+    if (rank_history_dirs.size() <= 1U) {
+        return reconstruct_solution_from_history(local_history, solved_meta, solved_depth);
+    }
+    if (solved_depth == 0U) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth must be positive");
+    }
+    if (solved_depth > local_history.depth_files.size() + 1U) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth exceeds stored local candidate history");
+    }
+
+    ReconstructedSolution solution;
+    solution.moves.resize(solved_depth);
+    solution.parent_indices.resize(solved_depth);
+
+    HistoryEntry cursor{solved_meta.parent_idx, solved_meta.route_packed, 0U};
+    std::uint32_t cursor_rank = unpack_source_rank(cursor.route_packed);
+    std::uint64_t parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
+    for (std::uint32_t depth = solved_depth; depth > 0; --depth) {
+        if (cursor_rank >= rank_history_dirs.size()) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("solution history source rank exceeds WORLD_SIZE");
+        }
+        const std::uint32_t out = depth - 1U;
+        solution.moves[out] = unpack_move(cursor.route_packed);
+        solution.parent_indices[out] = parent_idx;
+        if (out == 0U) {
+            break;
+        }
+        cursor = cursor_rank == local_rank
+            ? local_history.read_entry(out - 1U, parent_idx)
+            : read_history_entry_from_dir(rank_history_dirs[cursor_rank], out - 1U, parent_idx);
+        cursor_rank = unpack_source_rank(cursor.route_packed);
+        parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
+    }
+    return solution;
+}

# Multi-GPU WORLD_SIZE=2 Local Verification 2026-05-25

## Scope

- Implemented one C++/CUDA/NCCL solver path for `WORLD_SIZE=1` and `WORLD_SIZE=2`.
- No new git branch was created.
- Launcher responsibility remains only process startup; C++ reads `RANK`, `LOCAL_RANK`, `WORLD_SIZE`, calls `cudaSetDevice(LOCAL_RANK)`, and creates NCCL communicator through `BEAM_NCCL_ID_FILE`.

## Code Changes

- `tools/production_runner.cu`: added NCCL file rendezvous, env rank parsing, rank-local CUDA device selection, global stop propagation, rank-specific history dirs, and multi-rank history pruning disable.
- `cuda/dispatcher.cu`: added synchronized Stream5 exchange rounds, global threshold use, multi-rank FinalRequest/FinalResponse exchange, load-balanced local frontier materialization, and target-indexed `CandidateMeta` history exchange.
- `cuda/stream5.cu`: added generic u64 NCCL payload exchange and CUDA error checks for async copies.
- `cuda/final_materialize.cu`: added response-only materialization and response scatter helpers for multi-rank final exchange.
- `cuda/threshold.cu`: added multi-rank final phase counting and exact load-balance filtering.
- `kaggle/beam_kernel.ipynb`: converted from single-process `WORLD_SIZE=2` argument probe to Python launcher that starts `WORLD_SIZE` C++ processes with rank env vars.

## Verification

- Build: `docker compose run --rm beam-tests bash -lc "cmake --build build-docker --target production_runner"` passed.
- Target CUDA tests: `stream5_cuda_tests`, `final_cuda_tests`, `threshold_cuda_tests`, `dispatcher_cuda_tests`, `static_memory_cuda_tests` passed `5/5`.
- WORLD_SIZE=1 smoke: `BEAM_GPU_HEADROOM_BYTES=0 BEAM_HISTORY_MODE=ram ./build-docker/production_runner 0 1 4194304` passed; output included `puzzle_solved=0 puzzle_id=0`.
- Local WORLD_SIZE=2 runtime test was not possible because Docker sees one GPU: `0, NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3070 Laptop GPU, 8192 MiB`.

## Notes

- `production_runner 0 1 1024` currently fails before solver execution because auto-config rejects too-small beam layouts under the current final-layout budget model; `beam=4194304` fits and executes.
- Real `WORLD_SIZE=2` execution must be verified on Kaggle 2xT4 after the current source is available to the Kaggle notebook clone.

        if (mode == CandidateHistoryMode::Ram) {
            if (depth_index >= ram_depths.size() || !ram_ready[depth_index]) {
                throw std::runtime_error("candidate history RAM depth not materialized");
            }
            return ram_depths[depth_index][static_cast<std::size_t>(index)];
        }
        std::ifstream file(depth_files[depth_index], std::ios::binary);
        if (!file) {
            throw std::runtime_error("cannot open candidate history file for read: " + depth_files[depth_index].string());
        }
        const std::uint64_t offset = index * sizeof(HistoryEntry);
        file.seekg(static_cast<std::streamoff>(offset), std::ios::beg);
        HistoryEntry entry{};
        file.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(&entry), sizeof(entry));
        if (!file) {
            throw std::runtime_error("candidate history read failed: " + depth_files[depth_index].string());
        }
        return entry;
    }
};

struct ReconstructedSolution {
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> moves;
    std::vector<std::uint64_t> parent_indices;
};

ReconstructedSolution reconstruct_solution_from_history(
    const CpuCandidateHistory& history,
    const CandidateMeta& solved_meta,
    std::uint32_t solved_depth) {
    if (solved_depth == 0U) {
        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth must be positive");
    }
    if (solved_depth > history.depth_files.size() + 1U) {
        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth exceeds stored candidate history");
    }
    ReconstructedSolution solution;
    solution.moves.resize(solved_depth);
    solution.parent_indices.resize(solved_depth);

    HistoryEntry cursor{solved_meta.parent_idx, solved_meta.route_packed, 0U};
    std::uint64_t parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
    for (std::uint32_t depth = solved_depth; depth > 0; --depth) {
        const std::uint32_t out = depth - 1U;
        solution.moves[out] = unpack_move(cursor.route_packed);
        solution.parent_indices[out] = parent_idx;
        if (out == 0U) {
            break;
        }
        cursor = history.read_entry(out - 1U, parent_idx);
        parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
    }
    return solution;
}

State128 apply_solution_moves(
    const State128& initial,
    const std::vector<std::uint8_t>& moves,
    const std::vector<std::uint8_t>& generators) {
    State128 state = initial;
    clear_state_padding(state);

+ReconstructedSolution reconstruct_solution_from_rank_histories(
+    const CpuCandidateHistory& local_history,
+    const std::vector<std::filesystem::path>& rank_history_dirs,
+    std::uint32_t local_rank,
+    const CandidateMeta& solved_meta,
+    std::uint32_t solved_depth) {
+    if (rank_history_dirs.size() <= 1U) {
+        return reconstruct_solution_from_history(local_history, solved_meta, solved_depth);
+    }
+    if (solved_depth == 0U) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth must be positive");
+    }
+    if (solved_depth > local_history.depth_files.size() + 1U) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth exceeds stored local candidate history");
+    }
+
+    ReconstructedSolution solution;
+    solution.moves.resize(solved_depth);
+    solution.parent_indices.resize(solved_depth);
+
+    HistoryEntry cursor{solved_meta.parent_idx, solved_meta.route_packed, 0U};
+    std::uint32_t cursor_rank = unpack_source_rank(cursor.route_packed);
+    std::uint64_t parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
+    for (std::uint32_t depth = solved_depth; depth > 0; --depth) {
+        if (cursor_rank >= rank_history_dirs.size()) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("solution history source rank exceeds WORLD_SIZE");
+        }
+        const std::uint32_t out = depth - 1U;
+        solution.moves[out] = unpack_move(cursor.route_packed);
+        solution.parent_indices[out] = parent_idx;
+        if (out == 0U) {
+            break;
+        }
+        cursor = cursor_rank == local_rank
+            ? local_history.read_entry(out - 1U, parent_idx)
+            : read_history_entry_from_dir(rank_history_dirs[cursor_rank], out - 1U, parent_idx);
+        cursor_rank = unpack_source_rank(cursor.route_packed);
+        parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
+    }
+    return solution;
+}

# Multi-GPU WORLD_SIZE=2 Local Verification 2026-05-25

## Scope

- Implemented one C++/CUDA/NCCL solver path for `WORLD_SIZE=1` and `WORLD_SIZE=2`.
- No new git branch was created.
- Launcher responsibility remains only process startup; C++ reads `RANK`, `LOCAL_RANK`, `WORLD_SIZE`, calls `cudaSetDevice(LOCAL_RANK)`, and creates NCCL communicator through `BEAM_NCCL_ID_FILE`.

## Code Changes

- `tools/production_runner.cu`: added NCCL file rendezvous, env rank parsing, rank-local CUDA device selection, global stop propagation, rank-specific history dirs, and multi-rank history pruning disable.
- `cuda/dispatcher.cu`: added synchronized Stream5 exchange rounds, global threshold use, multi-rank FinalRequest/FinalResponse exchange, load-balanced local frontier materialization, and target-indexed `CandidateMeta` history exchange.
- `cuda/stream5.cu`: added generic u64 NCCL payload exchange and CUDA error checks for async copies.
- `cuda/final_materialize.cu`: added response-only materialization and response scatter helpers for multi-rank final exchange.
- `cuda/threshold.cu`: added multi-rank final phase counting and exact load-balance filtering.
- `kaggle/beam_kernel.ipynb`: converted from single-process `WORLD_SIZE=2` argument probe to Python launcher that starts `WORLD_SIZE` C++ processes with rank env vars.

## Verification

- Build: `docker compose run --rm beam-tests bash -lc "cmake --build build-docker --target production_runner"` passed.
- Target CUDA tests: `stream5_cuda_tests`, `final_cuda_tests`, `threshold_cuda_tests`, `dispatcher_cuda_tests`, `static_memory_cuda_tests` passed `5/5`.
- WORLD_SIZE=1 smoke: `BEAM_GPU_HEADROOM_BYTES=0 BEAM_HISTORY_MODE=ram ./build-docker/production_runner 0 1 4194304` passed; output included `puzzle_solved=0 puzzle_id=0`.
- Local WORLD_SIZE=2 runtime test was not possible because Docker sees one GPU: `0, NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3070 Laptop GPU, 8192 MiB`.

## Notes

- `production_runner 0 1 1024` currently fails before solver execution because auto-config rejects too-small beam layouts under the current final-layout budget model; `beam=4194304` fits and executes.
- Real `WORLD_SIZE=2` execution must be verified on Kaggle 2xT4 after the current source is available to the Kaggle notebook clone.

struct ReconstructedSolution {
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> moves;
    std::vector<std::uint64_t> parent_indices;
};

ReconstructedSolution reconstruct_solution_from_history(
    const CpuCandidateHistory& history,
    const CandidateMeta& solved_meta,
    std::uint32_t solved_depth) {
    if (solved_depth == 0U) {
        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth must be positive");
    }
    if (solved_depth > history.depth_files.size() + 1U) {
        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth exceeds stored candidate history");
    }
    ReconstructedSolution solution;
    solution.moves.resize(solved_depth);
    solution.parent_indices.resize(solved_depth);

    HistoryEntry cursor{solved_meta.parent_idx, solved_meta.route_packed, 0U};
    std::uint64_t parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
    for (std::uint32_t depth = solved_depth; depth > 0; --depth) {
        const std::uint32_t out = depth - 1U;
        solution.moves[out] = unpack_move(cursor.route_packed);
        solution.parent_indices[out] = parent_idx;
        if (out == 0U) {
            break;
        }
        cursor = history.read_entry(out - 1U, parent_idx);
        parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
    }
    return solution;
}

    clear_state_padding(state);

+HistoryEntry read_history_entry_from_dir(
+    const std::filesystem::path& history_dir,
+    std::uint32_t depth_index,
+    std::uint64_t index) {
+    const std::filesystem::path path = history_depth_path(history_dir, depth_index);
+    std::ifstream file(path, std::ios::binary);
+    if (!file) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("cannot open rank candidate history file for read: " + path.string());
+    }
+    const std::uint64_t offset = index * sizeof(HistoryEntry);
+    file.seekg(static_cast<std::streamoff>(offset), std::ios::beg);
+    HistoryEntry entry{};
+    file.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(&entry), sizeof(entry));
+    if (!file) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("rank candidate history read failed: " + path.string());
+    }
+    return entry;
+}
+
+ReconstructedSolution reconstruct_solution_from_rank_histories(
+    const CpuCandidateHistory& local_history,
+    const std::vector<std::filesystem::path>& rank_history_dirs,
+    std::uint32_t local_rank,
+    const CandidateMeta& solved_meta,
+    std::uint32_t solved_depth) {
+    if (rank_history_dirs.size() <= 1U) {
+        return reconstruct_solution_from_history(local_history, solved_meta, solved_depth);
+    }
+    if (solved_depth == 0U) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth must be positive");
+    }
+    if (solved_depth > local_history.depth_files.size() + 1U) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth exceeds stored local candidate history");
+    }
+
+    ReconstructedSolution solution;
+    solution.moves.resize(solved_depth);
+    solution.parent_indices.resize(solved_depth);
+
+    HistoryEntry cursor{solved_meta.parent_idx, solved_meta.route_packed, 0U};
+    std::uint32_t cursor_rank = unpack_source_rank(cursor.route_packed);
+    std::uint64_t parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
+    for (std::uint32_t depth = solved_depth; depth > 0; --depth) {
+        if (cursor_rank >= rank_history_dirs.size()) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("solution history source rank exceeds WORLD_SIZE");
+        }
+        const std::uint32_t out = depth - 1U;
+        solution.moves[out] = unpack_move(cursor.route_packed);
+        solution.parent_indices[out] = parent_idx;
+        if (out == 0U) {
+            break;
+        }
+        cursor = cursor_rank == local_rank
+            ? local_history.read_entry(out - 1U, parent_idx)
+            : read_history_entry_from_dir(rank_history_dirs[cursor_rank], out - 1U, parent_idx);
+        cursor_rank = unpack_source_rank(cursor.route_packed);
+        parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
+    }
+    return solution;
+}

Output:
# Multi-GPU WORLD_SIZE=2 Local Verification 2026-05-25

## Scope

- Implemented one C++/CUDA/NCCL solver path for `WORLD_SIZE=1` and `WORLD_SIZE=2`.
- No new git branch was created.
- Launcher responsibility remains only process startup; C++ reads `RANK`, `LOCAL_RANK`, `WORLD_SIZE`, calls `cudaSetDevice(LOCAL_RANK)`, and creates NCCL communicator through `BEAM_NCCL_ID_FILE`.

## Code Changes

- `tools/production_runner.cu`: added NCCL file rendezvous, env rank parsing, rank-local CUDA device selection, global stop propagation, rank-specific history dirs, and multi-rank history pruning disable.
- `cuda/dispatcher.cu`: added synchronized Stream5 exchange rounds, global threshold use, multi-rank FinalRequest/FinalResponse exchange, load-balanced local frontier materialization, and target-indexed `CandidateMeta` history exchange.
- `cuda/stream5.cu`: added generic u64 NCCL payload exchange and CUDA error checks for async copies.
- `cuda/final_materialize.cu`: added response-only materialization and response scatter helpers for multi-rank final exchange.
- `cuda/threshold.cu`: added multi-rank final phase counting and exact load-balance filtering.
- `kaggle/beam_kernel.ipynb`: converted from single-process `WORLD_SIZE=2` argument probe to Python launcher that starts `WORLD_SIZE` C++ processes with rank env vars.

## Verification

- Build: `docker compose run --rm beam-tests bash -lc "cmake --build build-docker --target production_runner"` passed.
- Target CUDA tests: `stream5_cuda_tests`, `final_cuda_tests`, `threshold_cuda_tests`, `dispatcher_cuda_tests`, `static_memory_cuda_tests` passed `5/5`.
- WORLD_SIZE=1 smoke: `BEAM_GPU_HEADROOM_BYTES=0 BEAM_HISTORY_MODE=ram ./build-docker/production_runner 0 1 4194304` passed; output included `puzzle_solved=0 puzzle_id=0`.
- Local WORLD_SIZE=2 runtime test was not possible because Docker sees one GPU: `0, NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3070 Laptop GPU, 8192 MiB`.

## Notes

- `production_runner 0 1 1024` currently fails before solver execution because auto-config rejects too-small beam layouts under the current final-layout budget model; `beam=4194304` fits and executes.
- Real `WORLD_SIZE=2` execution must be verified on Kaggle 2xT4 after the current source is available to the Kaggle notebook clone.

struct ReconstructedSolution {
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> moves;
    std::vector<std::uint64_t> parent_indices;
};

ReconstructedSolution reconstruct_solution_from_history(
    const CpuCandidateHistory& history,
    const CandidateMeta& solved_meta,
    std::uint32_t solved_depth) {
    if (solved_depth == 0U) {
        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth must be positive");
    }
    if (solved_depth > history.depth_files.size() + 1U) {
        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth exceeds stored candidate history");
    }
    ReconstructedSolution solution;
    solution.moves.resize(solved_depth);
    solution.parent_indices.resize(solved_depth);

    HistoryEntry cursor{solved_meta.parent_idx, solved_meta.route_packed, 0U};
    std::uint64_t parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
    for (std::uint32_t depth = solved_depth; depth > 0; --depth) {
        const std::uint32_t out = depth - 1U;
        solution.moves[out] = unpack_move(cursor.route_packed);
        solution.parent_indices[out] = parent_idx;
        if (out == 0U) {
            break;
        }
        cursor = history.read_entry(out - 1U, parent_idx);
        parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
    }
    return solution;
}

State128 apply_solution_moves(

    State128 state = initial;
    clear_state_padding(state);

+ReconstructedSolution reconstruct_solution_from_rank_histories(
+    const CpuCandidateHistory& local_history,
+    const std::vector<std::filesystem::path>& rank_history_dirs,
+    std::uint32_t local_rank,
+    const CandidateMeta& solved_meta,
+    std::uint32_t solved_depth) {
+    if (rank_history_dirs.size() <= 1U) {
+        return reconstruct_solution_from_history(local_history, solved_meta, solved_depth);
+    }
+    if (solved_depth == 0U) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth must be positive");
+    }
+    if (solved_depth > local_history.depth_files.size() + 1U) {
+        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth exceeds stored local candidate history");
+    }
+
+    ReconstructedSolution solution;
+    solution.moves.resize(solved_depth);
+    solution.parent_indices.resize(solved_depth);
+
+    HistoryEntry cursor{solved_meta.parent_idx, solved_meta.route_packed, 0U};
+    std::uint32_t cursor_rank = unpack_source_rank(cursor.route_packed);
+    std::uint64_t parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
+    for (std::uint32_t depth = solved_depth; depth > 0; --depth) {
+        if (cursor_rank >= rank_history_dirs.size()) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("solution history source rank exceeds WORLD_SIZE");
+        }
+        const std::uint32_t out = depth - 1U;
+        solution.moves[out] = unpack_move(cursor.route_packed);
+        solution.parent_indices[out] = parent_idx;
+        if (out == 0U) {
+            break;
+        }
+        cursor = cursor_rank == local_rank
+            ? local_history.read_entry(out - 1U, parent_idx)
+            : read_history_entry_from_dir(rank_history_dirs[cursor_rank], out - 1U, parent_idx);
+        cursor_rank = unpack_source_rank(cursor.route_packed);
+        parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
+    }
+    return solution;
+}

torchrun или Python launcher только запускает 2 C++ процесса.
C++ читает RANK/LOCAL_RANK/WORLD_SIZE.
C++ делает cudaSetDevice(LOCAL_RANK).
C++ сам создает NCCL communicator через file/TCP rendezvous.
Весь Stream5/finalization остается в C++.

Делай
Каждый GPU = отдельный rank/process: LOCAL_RANK, WORLD_SIZE.
Stream3 после dedup делит кандидаты по owner = hash % WORLD_SIZE.
Локальные кандидаты идут в local shard buffers.
Чужие кандидаты идут в remote_send_buffer.
Stream5 делает NCCL exchange CandidateMeta между rank.
После обмена полученные remote candidates собираются в локальные shard buffers.
Финал: local dedup -> NCCL AllReduce histogram -> global threshold -> load balance по rank -> FinalRequest/FinalResponse обмен -> новый local frontier.

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

[7] user: Да, торчран в самом начале просто запускает С++  мой.

torchrun или Python launcher только запускает 2 C++ процесса.
C++ читает RANK/LOCAL_RANK/WORLD_SIZE.
C++ делает cudaSetDevice(LOCAL_RANK).
C++ сам создает NCCL communicator через file/TCP rendezvous.
Весь Stream5/finalization остается в C++.

Делай
Каждый GPU = отдельный rank/process: LOCAL_RANK, WORLD_SIZE.
Stream3 после dedup делит кандидаты по owner = hash % WORLD_SIZE.
Локальные кандидаты идут в local shard buffers.
Чужие кандидаты идут в remote_send_buffer.
Stream5 делает NCCL exchange CandidateMeta между rank.
После обмена полученные remote candidates собираются в локальные shard buffers.
Финал: local dedup -> NCCL AllReduce histogram -> global threshold -> load balance по rank -> FinalRequest/FinalResponse обмен -> новый local frontier.

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

Full-beam depths 6-8 ran in `9.35333s`, `9.03494s`, and `9.23598s`; `stream4_jobs` dropped to `285/283/288`; `global_spill_peak=0`. Log saved as `test_results/docker_stream4_trigger_p0_d9_b8388608_trigger786432_2026-05-24.log`.
 - Ran local Docker `beam=16777216`, `depth_limit=60`, depth-logs-only build, manual config `SHARD_COUNT=4`, `SHARD_BUFFER_COUNT=2`, `STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES=196608`, `STREAM4_TRIGGER_CANDIDATES=786432`, `SHARD_CAPACITY_CANDIDATES=4194304`, `STREAM4_ACTIVE_SORT_SLOTS=2`, `GLOBAL_SPILL_CAPACITY=0`. Puzzle 0 solved at `depth=54` with `solution_length=55`, `elapsed_sec=1441.43`, `run_exit_code=0`. Full-beam depths mostly ran around `28-31s`, with one outlier `depth35=80.925s`. Log saved as `test_results/docker_depth60_p0_b16777216_depthlogs_only_2026-05-24.log`.
 - Published the working solver flow. GitHub `main` was updated to commit `4728a23` (`Add double-buffer Stream4 trigger runtime`). Kaggle kernel `trydotatwo/cayley-beam-gpu-runner` was pushed as public version 37 with notebook defaults `beam=2**24`, `depth_limit=60`, manual double-buffer config, and no tracked solution path injection. Kaggle API reported `KernelWorkerStatus.RUNNING` immediately after push.
+- Local memory probes for larger beams with double-buffer config and `SHARD_CAPACITY_CANDIDATES=4194304`: `beam=2**25`, `SHARD_COUNT=8` failed before allocation with required bytes `10561658544` versus budget `6658457600`; implied static allocation is `10471117312` bytes<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="159" />4194304_2026-05-24.log`.
+- Local single-rank memory probe simulating `100M` global beam on `2xT4` by using local beam `50000000`, `SHARD_COUNT=12`, `SHARD_CAPACITY_CANDIDATES=4194304`, and double-buffer mode reported required bytes `15700074160` per GPU. This is `14.62 GiB/GPU`, too close to a 16GB T4 practical usable budget after CUDA/NCCL/headroom. Log saved as `test_results/docker_memcheck_local50000000_shards12_cap4194304_2026-05-24.log`.
+- Kaggle version 40 tested the current notebook with `WORLD_SIZE=2` and `LOCAL_RANK=0` by passing the optional production runner args. The run reached `depth_start=0` and failed immediately at first finalization with `std::invalid_argument: single gpu finalization requires WORLD_SIZE=1 and LOCAL_RANK=0`; notebook recorded `return_code=-6`. Artifacts saved under `test_results/kaggle_v40_world_size2_output/` and report saved as `test_results/kaggle_v40_world_size2_report_2026-05-25.md`.
+- Implemented the first real multi-rank C++/CUDA/NCCL path without creating a new branch. `production_runner` now reads `WORLD_SIZE/RANK/LOCAL_RANK`, sets the CUDA device by `LOCAL_RANK`, creates NCCL communicators through `BEAM_NCCL_ID_FILE`, propagates global stop, and keeps Stream5/finalization in C++. Dispatcher now synchronizes Stream5 exchange rounds across ranks, handles zero-work ranks, performs global final threshold/counting, exchanges `FinalRequest`/`FinalResponse`, scatters load-balanced frontier states, and exchanges target-indexed `CandidateMeta` history.
+- Local verification: `cmake --build build-docker --target production_runner` passed; targeted CUDA tests `stream5/final/threshold/dispatcher/static_memory` passed `5/5`; `WORLD_SIZE=1` smoke with `BEAM_GPU_HEADROOM_BYTES=0 BEAM_HISTORY_MODE=ram production_runner 0 1 4194304` passed. Local `WORLD_SIZE=2` runtime test is blocked by one visible Docker GPU (`RTX 3070 Laptop GPU`). Report saved as `test_results/multi_gpu_world2_local_verification_2026-05-25.md`.

[7] user: Да, торчран в самом начале просто запускает С++  мой.

torchrun или Python launcher только запускает 2 C++ процесса.
C++ читает RANK/LOCAL_RANK/WORLD_SIZE.
C++ делает cudaSetDevice(LOCAL_RANK).
C++ сам создает NCCL communicator через file/TCP rendezvous.
Весь Stream5/finalization остается в C++.

Делай
Каждый GPU = отдельный rank/process: LOCAL_RANK, WORLD_SIZE.
Stream3 после dedup делит кандидаты по owner = hash % WORLD_SIZE.
Локальные кандидаты идут в local shard buffers.
Чужие кандидаты идут в remote_send_buffer.
Stream5 делает NCCL exchange CandidateMeta между rank.
После обмена полученные remote candidates собираются в локальные shard buffers.
Финал: local dedup -> NCCL AllReduce histogram -> global threshold -> load balance по rank -> FinalRequest/FinalResponse обмен -> новый local frontier.

[7] user: Да, торчран в самом начале просто запускает С++  мой.

torchrun или Python launcher только запускает 2 C++ процесса.
C++ читает RANK/LOCAL_RANK/WORLD_SIZE.
C++ делает cudaSetDevice(LOCAL_RANK).
C++ сам создает NCCL communicator через file/TCP rendezvous.
Весь Stream5/finalization остается в C++.

Делай
Каждый GPU = отдельный rank/process: LOCAL_RANK, WORLD_SIZE.
Stream3 после dedup делит кандидаты по owner = hash % WORLD_SIZE.
Локальные кандидаты идут в local shard buffers.
Чужие кандидаты идут в remote_send_buffer.
Stream5 делает NCCL exchange CandidateMeta между rank.
После обмена полученные remote candidates собираются в локальные shard buffers.
Финал: local dedup -> NCCL AllReduce histogram -> global threshold -> load balance по rank -> FinalRequest/FinalResponse обмен -> новый local frontier.

[7] user: Да, торчран в самом начале просто запускает С++  мой.

torchrun или Python launcher только запускает 2 C++ процесса.
C++ читает RANK/LOCAL_RANK/WORLD_SIZE.
C++ делает cudaSetDevice(LOCAL_RANK).
C++ сам создает NCCL communicator через file/TCP rendezvous.
Весь Stream5/finalization остается в C++.

Делай
Каждый GPU = отдельный rank/process: LOCAL_RANK, WORLD_SIZE.
Stream3 после dedup делит кандидаты по owner = hash % WORLD_SIZE.
Локальные кандидаты идут в local shard buffers.
Чужие кандидаты идут в remote_send_buffer.
Stream5 делает NCCL exchange CandidateMeta между rank.
После обмена полученные remote candidates собираются в локальные shard buffers.
Финал: local dedup -> NCCL AllReduce histogram -> global threshold -> load balance по rank -> FinalRequest/FinalResponse обмен -> новый local frontier.

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

[7] user: Да, торчран в самом начале просто запускает С++  мой.

torchrun или Python launcher только запускает 2 C++ процесса.
C++ читает RANK/LOCAL_RANK/WORLD_SIZE.
C++ делает cudaSetDevice(LOCAL_RANK).
C++ сам создает NCCL communicator через file/TCP rendezvous.
Весь Stream5/finalization остается в C++.

Делай
Каждый GPU = отдельный rank/process: LOCAL_RANK, WORLD_SIZE.
Stream3 после dedup делит кандидаты по owner = hash % WORLD_SIZE.
Локальные кандидаты идут в local shard buffers.
Чужие кандидаты идут в remote_send_buffer.
Stream5 делает NCCL exchange CandidateMeta между rank.
После обмена полученные remote candidates собираются в локальные shard buffers.
Финал: local dedup -> NCCL AllReduce histogram -> global threshold -> load balance по rank -> FinalRequest/FinalResponse обмен -> новый local frontier.

from pathlib import Path
import os
import shutil
import subprocess

WORK_DIR = Path('/kaggle/working')
TMP_DIR = Path('/tmp')
REPO_DIR = TMP_DIR / 'beam_solver'
CUTLASS_DIR = TMP_DIR / 'cutlass'
BUILD_DIR = TMP_DIR / 'beam_build'

def run_checked(cmd, cwd=None, env=None):
    print('+ ' + ' '.join(map(str, cmd)))
    subprocess.run(list(map(str, cmd)), cwd=cwd, env=env, check=True)

for transient_dir in (REPO_DIR, BUILD_DIR):
    if transient_dir.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(transient_dir)
run_checked(['git', 'clone', '--branch', GITHUB_BRANCH, '--depth', '1', GITHUB_REPO_URL, REPO_DIR])

if not (CUTLASS_DIR / 'include').exists():
    if CUTLASS_DIR.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(CUTLASS_DIR)
    run_checked(['git', 'clone', '--depth', '1', 'https://github.com/NVIDIA/cutlass.git', CUTLASS_DIR])

depth_logs = 'ON' if ENABLE_DEPTH_LOGS else 'OFF'
debug_logs = 'ON' if ENABLE_DEBUG_LOGS else 'OFF'
debug_master = 'ON' if ENABLE_DEBUG else 'OFF'
debug_stream_timing = 'ON' if DEBUG_STREAM_TIMING else 'OFF'
debug_inference_trace = 'ON' if DEBUG_INFERENCE_TRACE else 'OFF'
debug_path_trace = 'O<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1140" />up(5) if solved else '',
        'return_code': max(codes),
        'log_path': joined_logs,
    }

results = []
for index, puzzle_id in enumerate(range(START_PUZZLE_ID, START_PUZZLE_ID + PUZZLE_COUNT), start=1):
    result = run_puzzle(puzzle_id, index)
    results.append(result)
    if PUZZLE_LOG_EVERY and (index % PUZZLE_LOG_EVERY == 0):
        print(f'puzzle_progress={index}/{PUZZLE_COUNT} puzzle_id={puzzle_id} solved={result["solved"]} seconds={result["seconds"]:.6f} length={result["length"]}')
    df = pd.DataFrame(results)
    df.to_csv(RESULTS_CSV, index=False)
    solved_df = df[df['solved'] == 1][['puzzle_id', 'solution']].rename(columns={'puzzle_id': 'initial_state_id', 'solution': 'path'})
    solved_df.to_csv(SUBMISSION_CSV, index=False)

pd.DataFrame(results)

from collections import Counter
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import pandas as pd

df = pd.read_csv(RESULTS_CSV) if RESULTS_CSV.exists() else pd.DataFrame(results)
solved = df[df['solved'] == 1].copy()
lengths = [int(x) for x in solved['length'].dropna().tolist()]
hist_path = WORK_DIR / 'solution_length_histogram.png'
if lengths:
    min_len = min(lengths)
    max_len = max(lengths)
    avg_len = sum(lengths) / len(lengths)
    mode_len = Counter(lengths).most_common(1)[0][0]
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 5))
    plt.hist(lengths, bins=range(min_len, max_len + 2), edgecolor='black')
    plt.xlabel('solution_length')
    plt.ylabel('solved_puzzle_count')
    plt.title('Solved puzzle solution lengths')
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(hist_path, dpi=160)
    print(f'solved_count={len(lengths)} total_count={PUZZLE_COUNT}')
    print(f'min_solution_length={min_len}')
    print(f'max_solution_length={max_len}')
    print(f'avg_solution_length={avg_len:.6f}')
    print(f'mode_solution_length={mode_len}')
    print(f'histogram_png={hist_path}')
else:
    print(f'solved_count=0 total_count={PUZZLE_COUNT}')
    print(f'histogram_png_not_created={hist_path}')

enum class CandidateHistoryMode : std::uint8_t {
    Ram,
    Disk
};

CandidateHistoryMode parse_history_mode() {
    const char* value = std::getenv("BEAM_HISTORY_MODE");
    if (value == nullptr || value[0] == '\0' || std::strcmp(value, "ram") == 0) {
        return CandidateHistoryMode::Ram;
    }
    if (std::strcmp(value, "disk") == 0) {
        return CandidateHistoryMode::Disk;
    }
    throw std::runtime_error("BEAM_HISTORY_MODE must be ram or disk");
}

const char* history_mode_name(CandidateHistoryMode mode) {
    return mode == CandidateHistoryMode::Ram ? "ram" : "disk";
}

        if (!file) {
            throw std::runtime_error("candidate history read failed: " + depth_files[depth_index].string());
        }
        return entry;
    }
};

struct ReconstructedSolution {
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> moves;
    std::vector<std::uint64_t> parent_indices;
};

ReconstructedSolution reconstruct_solution_from_history(
    const CpuCandidateHistory& history,
    const CandidateMeta& solved_meta,
    std::uint32_t solved_depth) {
    if (solved_depth == 0U) {
        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth must be positive");
    }
    if (solved_depth > history.depth_files.size() + 1U) {
        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth exceeds stored candidate history");
    }
    ReconstructedSolution solution;
    solution.moves.resize(solved_depth);
    solution.parent_indices.resize(solved_depth);

    HistoryEntry cursor{solved_meta.parent_idx, solved_meta.route_packed, 0U};
    std::uint64_t parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
    for (std::uint32_t depth = solved_depth; depth > 0; --depth) {
        const std::uint32_t out = depth - 1U;
        solution.moves[out] = unpack_move(cursor.route_packed);
        solution.parent_indices[out] = parent_idx;
        if (out == 0U) {
            break;

    if (solved_depth == 0U) {

    if (solved_depth > history.depth_files.size() + 1U) {

    solution.moves.resize(solved_depth);

        const std::uint32_t out = depth - 1U;
        solution.moves[out] = unpack_move(cursor.route_packed);
        solution.parent_indices[out] = parent_idx;
        if (out == 0U) {

        }
        cursor = history.read_entry(out - 1U, parent_idx);
        parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
    }
    return solution;
}

State128 apply_solution_moves(
    const State128& initial,
    const std::vector<std::uint8_t>& moves,
    const std::vector<std::uint8_t>& generators) {
    State128 state = initial;
    clear_state_padding(state);
    for (std::uint8_t move : moves) {
        state = apply_move_flat_host(state, generators, move);
    }
    return state;
}

struct SolvedSnapshot {
    bool found = false;
    std::uint32_t count = 0;
    std::uint32_t overflow = 0;
    std::vector<CandidateMeta> meta;
    std::vector<std::uint32_t> depth;
};

SolvedSnapshot read_solved_snapshot(const StaticDeviceMemory& memory, std::uint32_t capacity) {
    SolvedSnapshot snapshot;
    std::uint32_t flag = 0;
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&flag, memory.solved_flag, sizeof(flag), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
    snapshot.found = flag != 0U;
    if (!snapshot.found) {
        return snapshot;
    }
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&snapshot.count, memory.solved_count, sizeof(snapshot.count), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&snapshot.overflow, memory.solved_overflow, sizeof(snapshot.overflow), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
    const std::uint32_t stored = std::min(snapshot.count, capacity);
    snapshot.meta.resize(stored);
    snapshot.depth.resize(stored);
    if (stored != 0U) {
        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(
            snapshot.meta.data(),
            memory.solved_meta_list,
            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(stored) * sizeof(CandidateMeta),
            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(
            snapshot.depth.data(),
            memory.solved_depth_list,
            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(stored) * sizeof(std::uint32_t),
            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
    }
    return snapshot;
}

void write_solution_artifacts(
    std::uint64_t puzzle_id,
    std::uint32_t depth_limit,
    std::uint64_t beam,
    const ReconstructedSolution& solution,
    const std::vector<std::string>& move_names,
    const State128& final_state,
    bool valid,
    const CpuCandidateHistory& history,
    const SolvedSnapshot& solved) {

enum class CandidateHistoryMode : std::uint8_t {
    Ram,
    Disk
};

CandidateHistoryMode parse_history_mode() {
    const char* value = std::getenv("BEAM_HISTORY_MODE");
    if (value == nullptr || value[0] == '\0' || std::strcmp(value, "ram") == 0) {
        return CandidateHistoryMode::Ram;
    }
    if (std::strcmp(value, "disk") == 0) {
        return CandidateHistoryMode::Disk;
    }
    throw std::runtime_error("BEAM_HISTORY_MODE must be ram or disk");
}

const char* history_mode_name(CandidateHistoryMode mode) {
    return mode == CandidateHistoryMode::Ram ? "ram" : "disk";
}

        }
        return entry;
    }
};

struct ReconstructedSolution {
    std::vector<std::uint8_t> moves;
    std::vector<std::uint64_t> parent_indices;
};

ReconstructedSolution reconstruct_solution_from_history(
    const CpuCandidateHistory& history,
    const CandidateMeta& solved_meta,
    std::uint32_t solved_depth) {
    if (solved_depth == 0U) {
        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth must be positive");
    }
    if (solved_depth > history.depth_files.size() + 1U) {
        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth exceeds stored candidate history");
    }
    ReconstructedSolution solution;
    solution.moves.resize(solved_depth);
    solution.parent_indices.resize(solved_depth);

    HistoryEntry cursor{solved_meta.parent_idx, solved_meta.route_packed, 0U};
    std::uint64_t parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
    for (std::uint32_t depth = solved_depth; depth > 0; --depth) {
        const std::uint32_t out = depth - 1U;
        solution.moves[out] = unpack_move(cursor.route_packed);
        solution.parent_indices[out] = parent_idx;
        if (out == 0U) {
            break;

    const CpuCandidateHistory& history,
    const CandidateMeta& solved_meta,
    std::uint32_t solved_depth) {
    if (solved_depth == 0U) {
        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth must be positive");
    }
    if (solved_depth > history.depth_files.size() + 1U) {
        throw std::runtime_error("solved depth exceeds stored candidate history");
    }
    ReconstructedSolution solution;
    solution.moves.resize(solved_depth);
    solution.parent_indices.resize(solved_depth);

    HistoryEntry cursor{solved_meta.parent_idx, solved_meta.route_packed, 0U};
    std::uint64_t parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
    for (std::uint32_t depth = solved_depth; depth > 0; --depth) {
        const std::uint32_t out = depth - 1U;
        solution.moves[out] = unpack_move(cursor.route_packed);
        solution.parent_indices[out] = parent_idx;
        if (out == 0U) {
            break;
        }
        cursor = history.read_entry(out - 1U, parent_idx);
        parent_idx = cursor.parent_idx;
    }
    return solution;
}

State128 apply_solution_moves(
    const State128& initial,
    const std::vector<std::uint8_t>& moves,
    const std::vector<std::uint8_t>& generators) {
    State128 state = initial;
    clear_state_padding(state);
    for (std::uint8_t move : moves) {
        state = apply_move_flat_host(state, generators, move);
    }
    return state;
}

struct SolvedSnapshot {
    bool found = false;
    std::uint32_t count = 0;
    std::uint32_t overflow = 0;
    std::vector<CandidateMeta> meta;
    std::vector<std::uint32_t> depth;
};

SolvedSnapshot read_solved_snapshot(const StaticDeviceMemory& memory, std::uint32_t capacity) {
    SolvedSnapshot snapshot;
    std::uint32_t flag = 0;
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&flag, memory.solved_flag, sizeof(flag), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
    snapshot.found = flag != 0U;
    if (!snapshot.found) {
        return snapshot;
    }
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&snapshot.count, memory.solved_count, sizeof(snapshot.count), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
    BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&snapshot.overflow, memory.solved_overflow, sizeof(snapshot.overflow), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
    const std::uint32_t stored = std::min(snapshot.count, capacity);
    snapshot.meta.resize(stored);
    snapshot.depth.resize(stored);
    if (stored != 0U) {
        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(
            snapshot.meta.data(),
            memory.solved_meta_list,
            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(stored) * sizeof(CandidateMeta),
            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
        BEAM_CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(
            snapshot.depth.data(),
            memory.solved_depth_list,
            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(stored) * sizeof(std::uint32_t),
            cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
    }
    return snapshot;
}

- Implemented first multi-rank C++/CUDA path while preserving `WORLD_SIZE=1`.
